32576:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32629:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
32630:   (function installModalResilience(){
32631:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32632:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32633:       if(!ac)return;
32634:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32635:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32636:       e.preventDefault();
32637:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32638:     },true);
32639:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32640:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32641:       if(!root)return;
32642:       e.preventDefault();
32643:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32644:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32645:     });
32646:     try{
32647:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32648:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32649:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32650:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32651:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32652:       });
32653:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32654:     }catch(e){}
32655:   }());

32503:   function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;')}
32504:   function num(v){v=Number(v);return isFinite(v)?v:0}
32505:   function money(v){return num(v).toLocaleString('ar-EG',{maximumFractionDigits:2})}

32511:   function opClear(k){if(k)delete H.ops[k]}
32512:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}
32513:   async function q(view,payload){var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_query',{p_view:view,p_payload:payload||{}});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل قراءة HR');return r.data}

32517:   function card(title,sub,body,actions){return '<section class="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden"><div class="px-6 py-5 bg-slate-50/80 border-b flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h3 class="font-black text-slate-800">'+esc(title)+'</h3><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">'+esc(sub||'')+'</p></div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">'+(actions||'')+'</div></div><div class="p-6">'+body+'</div></section>'}
32518:   function stat(title,value,icon,cls){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-5 shadow-sm"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="text-2xl font-black mt-2">'+esc(value)+'</div></div><div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center '+(cls||'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700')+'"><i class="fas '+icon+'"></i></div></div></div>'}

32522:   function textarea(label,id,value){return '<label class="block"><span class="block text-xs font-black text-slate-600 mb-2">'+esc(label)+'</span><textarea id="'+esc(id)+'" class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 min-h-[95px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-200">'+esc(value||'')+'</textarea></label>'}

32524:  function modal(title,body,onSubmit,key){

32530:   document.body.appendChild(r);

32533:   r.addEventListener('click',function(e){

32537:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));

32539:   });

32543:   }else{
32544:     E('rw-hr-form').onsubmit=async function(e){
32545:       e.preventDefault();
32546:       var save=e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
32547:       try{
32548:         if(save){
32549:           save.disabled=true;
32550:           save.textContent='جارٍ الحفظ…';
32551:         }
32552:         await onSubmit(key||'form:'+Date.now());
32553:       }catch(err){

32555:         if(save){
32556:           save.disabled=false;
32557:           save.textContent='حفظ';
32558:         }
32559:       }
32560:     };
32561:   }
32562: }
32563: function closeModal(){var r=E('rw-hr-modal-root');if(r)r.remove()}
32564:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
32565:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
32566:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
32567:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
32568:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}

32570:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
32571:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
32572:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
32573:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

32576:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32589:   async function disburseAdvance(id){
32590:     var tr=await supabase.from('treasury').select('id,account_name,account_code,type,current_balance,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).eq('is_active',true).in('type',['Cash','Bank']).order('account_name');
32591:     if(tr.error)throw tr.error;
32592:     var rows=tr.data||[];
32593:     if(!rows.length){toast('لا توجد خزينة أو حساب بنكي فعال للصرف','error');return;}
32594:     var opts=rows.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'">'+esc(x.account_name||x.account_code)+' · '+esc(x.type)+' · '+money(x.current_balance)+'</option>';}).join('');
32595:     var r=await Swal.fire({title:'صرف السلفة عبر سند Payments',html:'<div class="text-right text-sm text-slate-600 mb-2">سيتم إنشاء سند صرف مركزي وقيد محاسبي وربطه بالسلفة والموظف.</div><select id="hr-advance-treasury" class="swal2-select w-full">'+opts+'</select>',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'إنشاء سند الصرف',cancelButtonText:'إلغاء',preConfirm:function(){var t=E('hr-advance-treasury');if(!t||!t.value){Swal.showValidationMessage('اختر خزينة الصرف');return false;}return t.value;}});
32596:     if(!r.isConfirmed)return;
32597:     await c('advance.disburse',{advance_id:id,treasury_id:r.value},'disburse-advance:'+id);
32598:     toast('تم الصرف من خلال سند Payments مركزي وربطه بالسلفة','success');
32599:     return render();
32600:   }

32630:   (function installModalResilience(){
32631:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32632:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32633:       if(!ac)return;
32634:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32635:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32636:       e.preventDefault();
32637:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32638:     },true);
32639:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32640:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32641:       if(!root)return;
32642:       e.preventDefault();
32643:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32644:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32645:     });
32646:     try{
32647:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32648:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32649:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32650:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32651:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32652:       });
32653:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32654:     }catch(e){}
32655:   }());

30072: 
30073: function _exportReportCsv() {
30074:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
30075: 
30076:     if (!resultDiv) {
30077:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
30078:         return;
30079:     }
30080: 
30081:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
30082: 
30083:     if (!table) {
30084:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
30085:         return;
30086:     }
30087: 
30088:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
30089:     var csv = [];
30090: 
30091:     function csvCell(value) {
30092:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
30093:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
30094:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
30095: 
30096:         return '"' + s + '"';
30097:     }
30098: 
30099:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
30100:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
30101:         var line = [];
30102: 
30103:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
30104:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
30105:         }
30106: 
30107:         csv.push(line.join(','));

30154:         '<style>' +
30155:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
30156:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
30157:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +

30159:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
30160:         '</style>' +
30161:         '</head>' +
30162:         '<body>' +
30163:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
30164:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
30165:         '</body></html>';
30166: 
30167:     printWindow.document.open();
30168:     printWindow.document.write(html);
30169:     printWindow.document.close();
30170: }

32576:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32652:       });
32653:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32654:     }catch(e){}

32629:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
32630:   (function installModalResilience(){
32631:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32632:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32633:       if(!ac)return;
32634:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32635:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32636:       e.preventDefault();
32637:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32638:     },true);
32639:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32640:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32641:       if(!root)return;
32642:       e.preventDefault();
32643:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32644:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32645:     });
32646:     try{
32647:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32648:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32649:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32650:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32651:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32652:       });
32653:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32654:     }catch(e){}
32655:   }());

32660: // ============================================================
32661: // RW_CRM – إدارة علاقات العملاء (CRM)
32662: // ============================================================
32663: var RW_CRM = (function() {
32664:     'use strict';
32665: 
32666:     var state = {
32667:         customers: [],
32668:         assignees: [],
32669:         kpi: {},
32670:         search: '',
32671:         activeOnly: false,
32672:         searchTimer: null
32673:     };
32674: 
32675:     function _esc(s) {
32676:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
32677:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
32678:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
32679:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
32680:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
32681:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
32682:     }
32683: 
32684:     function _fmtNum(n) {
32685:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
32686:     }
32687: 
32688:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
32689:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
32690:     }
32691: 
32692:     function _today() {
32693:         var d = new Date();
32694:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
32695:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
32696:     }
32697: 
32698:     function _statusLabel(s) {
32699:         var map = {
32700:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
32701:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
32702:             completed: 'مكتملة',
32703:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
32704:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
32705:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
32706:         };
32707:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
32708:     }
32709: 
32710:     function _statusClass(s) {
32711:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
32712:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
32713:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
32714:     }
32715: 
32716:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
32717:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
32718:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
32719:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
32720:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
32721:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
32722:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
32723:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
32724:     }
32725: 
32726:     async function _loadDirectory() {
32727:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
32728:             p_search: state.search || null,
32729:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
32730:             p_limit: 200,
32731:             p_offset: 0
32732:         });
32733:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
32734: 
32735:         var payload = res.data || {};
32736:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
32737:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
32738:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
32739:         return payload;
32740:     }
32741: 
32742:     function _renderKpis() {
32743:         var k = state.kpi || {};
32744:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
32745:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
32746:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
32747:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
32748:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
32749:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
32750:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
32751:         '</div>';
32752:     }
32753: 
32754:     function _renderTable() {
32755:         if (!state.customers.length) {
32756:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
32757:         }
32758: 
32759:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
32760:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
32761:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
32762:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
32763:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
32764:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
32765:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
32766:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
32767:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
32768:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
32769:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
32770: 
32771:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
32772:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
32773:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
32774:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
32775: 
32776:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
32777:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
32778:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
32779:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
32780:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
32781:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
32782:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
32783:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
32784:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
32785:             '</tr>';
32786:         }
32787: 
32788:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
32789:     }
32790: 
32791:     function _bindDirectory() {
32792:         var search = byId('crm-search');
32793:         if (search) {
32794:             search.value = state.search;
32795:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
32796:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
32797:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
32798:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
32799:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
32800:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
32801:                         _bindOpen360();
32802:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
32803:                     }).catch(function(e) {
32804:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
32805:                     });
32806:                 }, 250);
32807:             });
32808:         }

32512:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}

32575:   async function employeesTab(cn){await loadPeople();var rows=H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'});cn.innerHTML=card('دليل الموظفين','Employee 360 من مركز واحد','<div class="flex gap-2 mb-5"><input id="hr-emp-search" class="flex-1 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border" placeholder="بحث بالاسم أو البريد أو الرقم أو الوظيفة">'+btn('ملف موظف','new-profile')+'</div><div id="hr-emp-grid" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">'+rows.map(function(e){var total=num(e.basic_salary)+num(e.housing_allowance)+num(e.transport_allowance)+num(e.other_allowance)-num(e.default_deduction);return '<article data-eid="'+esc(e.id)+'" class="p-5 bg-white border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md"><div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-black">'+esc((e.name||'?')[0])+'</div><div class="min-w-0"><div class="font-black truncate">'+esc(e.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</div></div></div><div class="mt-4">'+employeeMeta(e)+'</div><div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t flex justify-between text-sm"><span class="text-slate-500">التعويض الحالي</span><b class="text-indigo-700">'+money(total)+' EGP</b></div></article>'}).join('')+'</div>');var s=E('hr-emp-search');if(s)s.oninput=function(){var v=s.value.toLowerCase();cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){var e=rows.filter(function(x){return x.id===el.getAttribute('data-eid')})[0]||{};var h=[e.name,e.email,e.employee_number,e.job_title,e.department_name,e.position_name].join(' ').toLowerCase();el.style.display=!v||h.indexOf(v)>-1?'':'none'})};cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){el.onclick=function(){open360(el.getAttribute('data-eid'))}})}
32576:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32586:  var financialBody='<div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-5">'+stat('إجمالي المديونية على الموظفين',money(debt)+' ج.م','fa-file-invoice-dollar','bg-amber-50 text-amber-700')+stat('مستحقات الموظفين لدى الشركة',money(payable)+' ج.م','fa-hand-holding-usd','bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700')+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">الأرصدة من القيود المرحلة فقط. الموجب على الموظف، والسالب مستحق له. طلبات السلف غير المصروفة لا تدخل في هذه الأرقام.</div>'+table(['الموظف','الحالة','طبيعة الذمة','الحساب','إجمالي المدين','إجمالي الدائن','الرصيد واتجاهه'],financial.map(function(x){var b=num(x.outstanding_balance),dir=b>0?badge('على الموظف','warn'):b<0?badge('مستحق للموظف','ok'):badge('متزن','muted');return tr([esc(x.employee_name||'-'),esc(x.employee_status||'-'),esc(types[x.party_nature]||x.party_nature||'-'),esc((x.account_code||'')+' — '+(x.account_name||'')),money(x.total_debit)+' ج.م',money(x.total_credit)+' ج.م',dir+' <b class="mr-2">'+money(Math.abs(b))+' ج.م</b>'])}));

32590:     var tr=await supabase.from('treasury').select('id,account_name,account_code,type,current_balance,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).eq('is_active',true).in('type',['Cash','Bank']).order('account_name');
32591:     if(tr.error)throw tr.error;
32592:     var rows=tr.data||[];
32593:     if(!rows.length){toast('لا توجد خزينة أو حساب بنكي فعال للصرف','error');return;}
32594:     var opts=rows.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'">'+esc(x.account_name||x.account_code)+' · '+esc(x.type)+' · '+money(x.current_balance)+'</option>';}).join('');
32595:     var r=await Swal.fire({title:'صرف السلفة عبر سند Payments',html:'<div class="text-right text-sm text-slate-600 mb-2">سيتم إنشاء سند صرف مركزي وقيد محاسبي وربطه بالسلفة والموظف.</div><select id="hr-advance-treasury" class="swal2-select w-full">'+opts+'</select>',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'إنشاء سند الصرف',cancelButtonText:'إلغاء',preConfirm:function(){var t=E('hr-advance-treasury');if(!t||!t.value){Swal.showValidationMessage('اختر خزينة الصرف');return false;}return t.value;}});
32596:     if(!r.isConfirmed)return;
32597:     await c('advance.disburse',{advance_id:id,treasury_id:r.value},'disburse-advance:'+id);
32598:     toast('تم الصرف من خلال سند Payments مركزي وربطه بالسلفة','success');
32599:     return render();
32600:   }

32628:   function realtime(){try{if(H.channel)supabase.removeChannel(H.channel);var refresh=function(){clearTimeout(H.timer);H.timer=setTimeout(function(){render()},700)};var tables=['employee_profiles','employee_attendance','employee_leave_requests','employee_documents','hr_departments','hr_positions','hr_employee_assignments','hr_employee_schedule_assignments','hr_work_schedules','hr_attendance_events','hr_work_entries','hr_leave_types','hr_leave_balances','hr_requests','hr_request_approvals','hr_salary_advances','hr_salary_components','hr_contracts','hr_contract_components','hr_payroll_periods','hr_payroll_runs','hr_payslips','hr_payslip_lines','hr_payroll_accounting_map'];H.channel=supabase.channel('rw-hr-mother-final');tables.forEach(function(t){H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:t},refresh)});H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:'journal_lines',filter:'party_type=eq.Employee'},refresh);H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:'cash_box',filter:'party_type=eq.Employee'},refresh);H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'UPDATE',schema:'public',table:'journal_entries',filter:'status=eq.Posted'},refresh);H.channel.subscribe()}catch(e){console.warn('RW_HR realtime',e)}}
32629:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
32630:   (function installModalResilience(){
32631:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32632:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32633:       if(!ac)return;
32634:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32635:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32636:       e.preventDefault();
32637:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32638:     },true);
32639:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32640:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32641:       if(!root)return;
32642:       e.preventDefault();
32643:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32644:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32645:     });
32646:     try{
32647:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32648:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32649:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32650:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32651:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32652:       });
32653:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32654:     }catch(e){}
32655:   }());
